 ca_prospecting.1.b: "Provide adequate funding and supplies."
 ca_prospecting.1.c: "Spare no expense. Bring the best experts!"

 ca_prospecting.2.t: "Disaster in the Highlands"
 ca_prospecting.2.d: "Tragedy has struck our expedition. Heavy rains triggered a massive mudslide that not only buried our equipment and delayed our survey, but also permanently ruined several fertile terraces cultivated by local farmers."
 ca_prospecting.2.a: "A terrible setback."

 ca_prospecting.3.t: "Clearing the Path to Progress"
 ca_prospecting.3.d: "While carving paths through the dense vegetation for our geologists, the expedition has inadvertently cleared large tracts of highly fertile land. Local farmers are already rushing in to plant new crops."
 ca_prospecting.3.a: "A fortunate side effect!"

 ca_prospecting.4.t: "Local Resistance"
 ca_prospecting.4.d: "Rural communities have barricaded the roads, fearful that the arrival of state prospectors means the seizure of their ancestral lands for mining operations."
 ca_prospecting.4.a: "Disperse them! Progress cannot be halted."
 ca_prospecting.4.b: "Halt the survey. We must negotiate."

 ca_prospecting.5.t: "A Deep Vein Discovered"
 ca_prospecting.5.d: "Our geologists have struck the motherlode! However, the vein runs deep below the water table. Simple picks and shovels will no longer suffice; we will need industrial pumps to extract this wealth."
 ca_prospecting.5.a: "Mandate the use of heavy machinery."
 ca_prospecting.5.b: "Note the location for future exploitation."

 ca_prospecting.6.t: "Local Gold Rush"
 ca_prospecting.6.d: "News of gold nuggets found in a local river has spread like wildfire. Thousands of peasants are abandoning their harvests, rushing to the hills with pans and pickaxes in hopes of striking it rich."
 ca_prospecting.6.a: "There's gold in those hills!"

 ca_prospecting.7.t: "The Foreign Consortium"
 ca_prospecting.7.d: "A wealthy consortium of foreign investors has approached our government. They offer to fully finance the remainder of our geological survey, provided they are granted exclusive tax-exempt rights to the most lucrative mines."
 ca_prospecting.7.a: "Accept their capital. We need the progress."
 ca_prospecting.7.b: "Reject them. Our soil belongs to our people."

 ca_prospecting.8.t: "Missing Funds"
 ca_prospecting.8.d: "A scandalous audit has revealed that a significant portion of the survey's budget has been embezzled by a regional governor and his landowning cronies to buy private estates."
 ca_prospecting.8.a: "Arrest the thieves and seize the assets!"
 ca_prospecting.8.b: "We cannot afford a political crisis. Ignore it."

 ca_prospecting.9.t: "The Inaccessible Vein"
 ca_prospecting.9.d: "An immense deposit of minerals has been mapped, but it sits atop a jagged mountain range surrounded by ravines. Extracting it will be an absolute logistical nightmare."
 ca_prospecting.9.a: "We will need railways to move this."

 ca_prospecting.10.t: "Jungle Fever"
 ca_prospecting.10.d: "The expedition has entered the deep rainforests. Malaria and yellow fever are wreaking havoc among our porters, and morale is practically non-existent."
 ca_prospecting.10.a: "Call them back. It's a death trap."
 ca_prospecting.10.b: "Push forward! Replace those who fall."

 ca_prospecting.11.t: "Ancient Ruins Discovered"
 ca_prospecting.11.d: "Instead of mineral wealth, our prospectors have stumbled upon a massive, overgrown complex of ancient Mayan ruins. It holds no economic value, but the academic world is mesmerized."
 ca_prospecting.11.a: "A testament to our glorious past."

 ca_prospecting.12.t: "Fool's Gold"
 ca_prospecting.12.d: "What was initially celebrated as the largest gold discovery of the century has turned out to be nothing but iron pyrite. The nation is the laughing stock of the international press."
 ca_prospecting.12.a: "A humiliating mistake."

 ca_prospecting.13.t: "Porters on Strike"
 ca_prospecting.13.d: "The local porters carrying the heavy surveying equipment are refusing to move another inch unless their wages are doubled immediately."
 ca_prospecting.13.a: "Pay them what they ask."
 ca_prospecting.13.b: "Fire them all! We'll find others."

 ca_prospecting.14.t: "Subsoil Rights Conflict"
 ca_prospecting.14.d: "Powerful landowners are claiming that the minerals found beneath their haciendas belong entirely to them, challenging the state's authority over the subsoil."
 ca_prospecting.14.a: "Grant them private mining rights."
 ca_prospecting.14.b: "The subsoil is the absolute property of the State!"

 ca_prospecting.15.t: "The Church's Blessing"
 ca_prospecting.15.d: "Local priests have enthusiastically joined the expedition, using their knowledge of the remote parishes to guide our geologists to unexplored caves and riverbeds."
 ca_prospecting.15.a: "God guides our picks."

 ca_prospecting.16.t: "Geological Innovation"
 ca_prospecting.16.d: "Necessity is the mother of invention. Our surveyors have developed a brilliant new technique for rapid mineral cataloging in tropical environments."
 ca_prospecting.16.a: "Publish these findings!"

 ca_prospecting.17.t: "Black Gold"
 ca_prospecting.17.d: "Excellent news! Massive, highly accessible coal seams have been discovered. This will undoubtedly become the fuel for our future industrialization."
 ca_prospecting.17.a: "The steam age is upon us."

 ca_prospecting.18.t: "The Geological Spy"
 ca_prospecting.18.d: "Our guards have caught a man making covert copies of our geological maps. He confesses to being a spy hired by a foreign power eager to know our resource capabilities."
 ca_prospecting.18.a: "Arrest him quietly."
 ca_prospecting.18.b: "He escaped before we could secure the maps!"

 # ==========================================
 # COUNTRY SELECTION SCREEN FLAVOR TEXT (1836)
 # ==========================================

 # UCA - United Provinces of Central America
 UCA_FLAVOR_TEXT:0 "The grand dream of a united Central America is fraying at the edges. Since gaining independence, the Federal Republic has been plagued by bitter struggles between liberal reformers in the capital and conservative strongholds in the provinces. As 1836 dawns, the federal government faces empty coffers, regional rebellions, and the looming shadow of dissolution. Can you hold the fragile union together against the tides of separatism, or will the provinces shatter into independent republics?"

 # RDT - Talamanca (Bribri / Indigenous Autonomy)
 RDT_FLAVOR_TEXT:0 "Deep within the impenetrable rainforests and rugged mountains of the south, the indigenous peoples of Talamanca have preserved their autonomy and ancient traditions for centuries. Resisting both Spanish conquistadors and the newly formed Central American states, the Bribri and Cabécar tribes stand as fierce defenders of their sovereign lands. In a rapidly industrializing world hungry for resources, you must guide your people to adapt, resist, and survive the encroaching forces of colonialism."

# CIP - Soconusco (Disputed Neutral Territory)
 CIP_FLAVOR_TEXT:0 "Soconusco lies at the fractured border between the Mexican Republic and the Federal Republic of Central America. Following the collapse of the First Mexican Empire, while Chiapas was annexed by Mexico, the vital and wealthy region of Soconusco bravely declared its absolute neutrality. Now, as a highly disputed territory coveted by both northern and southern powers, this rich agricultural enclave must navigate treacherous diplomatic waters. As 1836 dawns, will Soconusco maintain its precarious autonomy, or will it be forced to choose a side as the isthmus descends into chaos?"
